## [MIDI Project] `Michael_Jackson disco`

### Project README (`Michael_Jackson disco/readme.md`)
```markdown
This is a disco sample track in the classic Michael Jackson style.
This is not instrumental music; the melody track is something I composed myself, but it's actually a beat with an added melody on top — so that when a user later composes their own melody and needs you to make the beat, you can mix/harmonize accordingly.
```

## [MIDI Project] `ballad song template`

### Project README (`ballad song template/readme.md`)
```markdown
This is a pop ballad, template
This is not instrumental music; the melody track is something I composed myself, but it's actually a beat with an added melody on top — so that when a user later composes their own melody and needs you to make the beat, you can mix/harmonize accordingly
Tempo: 60
Key: Cm, C#m when modulating up
From bar 1 to bar 7, free fill, no fixed tempo
The warm, solemn opening builds up to a soaring open chorus
Bar 8, open chorus
First 6 bars, build tension with sustained pounding and tutti, combined with fast drum fills
And the final section, transition
Remaining bars of the open chorus, into the groove, played normally
Full instrumentation including: grand piano, strings, acoustic guitar, bass, drums
Bar 18, free intro
At first, piano only; later, add other instruments such as strings, lead guitar, bass, timpani, cymbal rolls, and so on
Bar 26, verse 1
Gentle, piano only; after a while, add a touch of strings to lead into verse 2
The last 2 bars of verse 1 are the transition leading into verse 2
Bar 36, verse 2 — at this point, drums, bass, and guitar have come in
A few bars later, add a bit of strings so the verse doesn't get boring, conveniently leading into the chorus
The last 2 bars of verse 2 are the transition leading into the chorus
Bar 46, chorus
Climactic — all instruments come in together
The last bar of the chorus is the transition into the bridge:
Bridge, bar 55
Everything gradually builds to a climax with a guitar solo, played more intensely, with a bit of fast drum fill
Bar 61, chorus in the new key
The first 4 bars have no groove — instead, sustained pounding and tutti with fast drum fills
Then, the groove comes in
Bar 70, outro, piano alone
Gentle
```

## [MIDI Project] `my_ballad_beat`

### Project README (`my_ballad_beat/readme.md`)
```markdown
This is a pop ballad project with a deeply melancholic mood; the chords lean toward a US-UK style. Everything is very gentle until the key change—right before the modulation, I added two bars for a fast drum fill, and then the key changes. The climax defines the pain. This song is in a major key, with a tempo of 70.
```
